'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { MODELS } from '../lib/models';

const won=(n:number)=>new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR',{maximumFractionDigits:6}).format(n)+'원';
async function call(path:string,body?:unknown,method='POST') {
 const r=await fetch('/api/'+path,body?{method,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)}:undefined);
 const data:any=await r.json();if(!r.ok)throw new Error(data.error||'요청을 완료하지 못했습니다.');return data;
}
declare global { interface Window { TossPayments?: (key:string)=>{payment:(v:{customerKey:string})=>{requestPayment:(v:unknown)=>Promise<void>}} } }
let sdk:Promise<void>|undefined;
function loadToss(){return sdk||=new Promise<void>((resolve,reject)=>{
 if(window.TossPayments){resolve();return;}
 const s=document.createElement('script');s.src='https://js.tosspayments.com/v2/standard';s.onload=()=>resolve();s.onerror=()=>{sdk=undefined;s.remove();reject(new Error('결제창을 불러오지 못했습니다.'));};document.head.appendChild(s);
});}

export default function BillingPanel({admin=false}:{admin?:boolean}) {
 const [data,setData]=useState<any>(null),[error,setError]=useState(''),[notice,setNotice]=useState(''),[busy,setBusy]=useState(false),[config,setConfig]=useState<any>(null);
 async function refresh(){setData(await call('billing'));if(admin){const r=await call('admin/billing');setConfig(r.config);}}
 useEffect(()=>{let active=true;(async()=>{
  try{
   const q=new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
   if(window.location.pathname==='/billing/success'){
    setBusy(true);
    await call('payments/confirm',{paymentKey:q.get('paymentKey'),orderId:q.get('orderId'),amount:Number(q.get('amount'))});
    if(active){setNotice('결제 확인이 완료되었습니다. 중복 확인으로 잔액이 두 번 늘어나지 않습니다.');window.history.replaceState(null,'','/billing');}
   }else if(window.location.pathname==='/billing/fail')setError('결제가 완료되지 않았습니다. 충전 내역을 확인하고 다시 시도하세요.');
   if(active)await refresh();
  }catch(e){if(active)setError((e as Error).message);}finally{if(active)setBusy(false);}
 })();return()=>{active=false;};},[admin]);
 async function checkout(amount:number){setBusy(true);setError('');try{
  const order=await call('payments/orders',{amount});await loadToss();
  if(!window.TossPayments)throw new Error('결제창을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  await window.TossPayments(order.clientKey).payment({customerKey:order.customerKey}).requestPayment({method:'CARD',amount:{currency:'KRW',value:order.amount},orderId:order.orderId,orderName:'Open Prism '+won(amount)+' 충전',successUrl:location.origin+'/billing/success',failUrl:location.origin+'/billing/fail'});
 }catch(e){setError((e as Error).message);}finally{setBusy(false);}}
 return <div className="page-inner billing-panel">
  <div className="page-heading"><span className="eyebrow">PREPAID AI WORKSPACE</span><h1>필요한 만큼 충전하고 사용하세요.</h1><p>월 구독 없이 1만·3만·5만원을 충전하고, 모델별 실제 사용량에 따라 잔액을 차감합니다.</p></div>
  {error&&<div className="error-banner" role="alert">{error}</div>}{notice&&<p role="status">{notice}</p>}
  {!data?(error?<p><a className="primary-button" href="/signin-with-chatgpt?return_to=%2Fbilling" target="_top">로그인하고 충전 내역 보기</a></p>:<p role="status">충전 정보를 확인하고 있습니다.</p>):<>
   <div className="stats-grid"><div className="stat-card"><div>사용 가능 잔액</div><strong>{won(data.balance/1e6)}</strong><small>진행 중인 요청의 예약금 제외</small></div><div className="stat-card"><div>충전 단위</div><strong>1만 · 3만 · 5만원</strong><small>자동 갱신·월 구독 없음</small></div><div className="stat-card"><div>결제 상태</div><strong>{data.mode==='disabled'?'준비 중':data.mode==='test'?'테스트':'실결제'}</strong><small>{data.mode==='test'?'테스트 잔액 '+won(data.testBalance/1e6)+' · 실제 AI 사용 불가':'차감 단가·출시 정책 확정 후 결제 활성화'}</small></div></div>
   <div className="model-grid">{data.topUps.map((amount:number)=><article className="model-card" key={amount}><h3>{won(amount)}</h3><p>{data.mode==='test'?'실제 금액이 청구되지 않는 테스트 결제입니다.':'선택한 금액만 충전합니다. 자동 결제는 없습니다.'}</p><button className="primary-button" disabled={busy||data.mode==='disabled'} onClick={()=>checkout(amount)}>{data.mode==='disabled'?'충전 준비 중':data.mode==='test'?'테스트 충전':'충전하기'}</button></article>)}</div>
   {data.blockers.length>0&&<section className="data-panel"><h2>출시 준비 현황</h2><ul>{data.blockers.map((b:string)=><li key={b}>{b}</li>)}</ul><p>위 항목과 결제 연결이 완료되기 전에는 실결제가 열리지 않습니다.</p></section>}
   <section className="data-panel"><h2>모델별 차감 단가</h2><p>단위: 100만 토큰당 원. 비교 응답은 선택한 모델마다 각각 차감합니다.</p>{Object.keys(data.rates).length?<div className="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>모델</th><th>입력</th><th>출력·추론</th></tr></thead><tbody>{Object.entries(data.rates).map(([id,r]:any)=><tr key={id}><td>{MODELS.find(m=>m.id===id)?.name||id}</td><td>{won(r.input)}</td><td>{won(r.output)}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>:<p>차감 단가가 미정입니다. 단가가 없는 모델의 유료 요청은 차단됩니다.</p>}<p>각 요청 전 최대 사용 비용을 예약하고, 확인된 사용량으로 정산한 차액을 돌려드립니다. 중단·오류로 사용량이 불확실하면 확인 전까지 예약금을 보류합니다. Pro 모드는 별도 단가 확정 전까지 비활성화됩니다.</p></section>
   <section className="data-panel"><h2>충전 내역</h2><div className="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>날짜</th><th>금액</th><th>구분</th><th>상태</th></tr></thead><tbody>{data.payments.map((p:any)=><tr key={p.id}><td>{new Date(p.created_at).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</td><td>{won(p.amount)}</td><td>{p.mode==='test'?'테스트':'실결제'}</td><td>{p.status==='paid'?'충전 완료':p.status==='review'?'정산 확인 필요':'결제 대기'}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table>{!data.payments.length&&<p>충전 내역이 없습니다.</p>}</div></section>

   <section className="data-panel"><h2>판매자 정보</h2><p>{data.business.name} · 대표자 {data.business.representative}</p><p>사업자등록번호: {data.business.registrationNumber||'미등록'}</p><p>사업장 주소: {data.business.address||'미등록'}</p><p>고객지원: {data.business.supportEmail||'미등록'}</p></section>
  </>}
  {admin&&config&&<section className="data-panel"><h2>출시 설정</h2><p>저장만으로 실결제가 켜지지 않습니다. 운영 환경의 별도 출시 설정과 결제키가 필요합니다.</p><form onSubmit={async e=>{e.preventDefault();setBusy(true);setError('');try{await call('admin/billing',config);await refresh();setNotice('설정을 저장했습니다.');}catch(e){setError((e as Error).message);}finally{setBusy(false);}}}>
   {(['registrationNumber','address','supportEmail','refundPolicy','validityPolicy'] as const).map((key,i)=><label className="billing-field" key={key}>{['사업자등록번호','사업장 주소','고객지원 이메일','환불 기준','충전금 유효기간'][i]}<input value={config[key]} maxLength={4000} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,[key]:e.target.value})}/></label>)}
   <h3>모델별 단가 (100만 토큰당 원)</h3>{MODELS.map(m=><div className="billing-rate" key={m.id}><span>{m.name}</span>{(['input','output'] as const).map((k,i)=><label key={k}>{i?'출력':'입력'}<input type="number" min="1" max="1000000" step="1" value={config.rates[m.id]?.[k]??''} onChange={e=>{const rates={...config.rates};const value=Number(e.target.value);if(e.target.value===''){delete rates[m.id];}else rates[m.id]={input:rates[m.id]?.input||value,output:rates[m.id]?.output||value,[k]:value};setConfig({...config,rates});}}/></label>)}</div>)}
   <button className="primary-button" disabled={busy}>설정 저장</button>
  </form></section>}
 </div>;
}

import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export type ChatGPTUser = {
  userId: string;
  displayName: string;
  email: string;
  fullName: string | null;
};

const USER_ID_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-id";
const USER_EMAIL_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-email";
const USER_FULL_NAME_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-full-name";
const USER_FULL_NAME_ENCODING_HEADER =
  "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding";
const PERCENT_ENCODED_UTF8 = "percent-encoded-utf-8";
const SIGN_IN_PATH = "/signin-with-chatgpt";
const SIGN_OUT_PATH = "/signout-with-chatgpt";
const CALLBACK_PATH = "/callback";

export async function getChatGPTUser(): Promise<ChatGPTUser | null> {
  const requestHeaders = await headers();
  const userId = requestHeaders.get(USER_ID_HEADER);
  const email = requestHeaders.get(USER_EMAIL_HEADER);
  if (!userId || !email) return null;

  const encodedFullName = requestHeaders.get(USER_FULL_NAME_HEADER);
  const fullName =
    encodedFullName &&
    requestHeaders.get(USER_FULL_NAME_ENCODING_HEADER) === PERCENT_ENCODED_UTF8
      ? safeDecodeURIComponent(encodedFullName)
      : null;

  return {
    userId,
    displayName: fullName ?? email,
    email,
    fullName,
  };
}

export async function requireChatGPTUser(
  returnTo: string,
): Promise<ChatGPTUser> {
  const user = await getChatGPTUser();
  if (user) return user;

  redirect(chatGPTSignInPath(returnTo));
}

export function chatGPTSignInPath(returnTo: string): string {
  const safeReturnTo = safeRelativeReturnPath(returnTo);
  return `${SIGN_IN_PATH}?return_to=${encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo)}`;
}

export function chatGPTSignOutPath(returnTo = "/"): string {
  const safeReturnTo = safeRelativeReturnPath(returnTo);
  return `${SIGN_OUT_PATH}?return_to=${encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo)}`;
}

function safeRelativeReturnPath(value: string): string {
  if (!value.startsWith("/") || value.startsWith("//")) return "/";

  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value, "https://app.local");
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
  if (url.origin !== "https://app.local") return "/";
  if (isReservedAuthPath(url.pathname)) return "/";

  return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
}

function isReservedAuthPath(pathname: string): boolean {
  return (
    pathname === SIGN_IN_PATH ||
    pathname === SIGN_OUT_PATH ||
    pathname === CALLBACK_PATH
  );
}

function safeDecodeURIComponent(value: string): string | null {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(value);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: build/connector-preview-plugin.mjs =====
UTF8_BYTES: 7409
SHA256: c85dddfed97753d80de89050b5a1316b2b490cdfab2faa119a3417cb6e6c315c

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { access, readFile, rename, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { setTimeout as delay } from "node:timers/promises";
import getRawBody from "raw-body";

const moduleId = "virtual:sites-connector-preview";
const resolvedId = `\0${moduleId}`;
const endpoint = "/__sites_connector_preview/invoke";
const contextEndpoint = "/__sites_connector_preview/context";
const unavailable = {
  status: "binding_unavailable",
  message:
    "Connected apps need an active agent preview session in this preview.",
};
const uncertain = {
  status: "upstream_error",
  message:
    "The app did not return a confirmed result. The action may have completed; check before trying again.",
};

// A development-only adapter. The shared project directory connects the preview
// process to its owning agent even when they use different network namespaces.
export function connectorPreview() {
  const bridgeToken = randomUUID();
  let root;
  let server;
  async function readSession() {
    const session = JSON.parse(
      await readFile(path.join(root, "session.json"), "utf8"),
    );
    if (
      !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/.test(session.id) ||
      (session.expiresAt !== null && !(session.expiresAt > Date.now()))
    )
      throw new Error("Session ended");
    if (
      !Number.isInteger(session.timeoutMs) ||
      session.timeoutMs <= 0 ||
      session.timeoutMs > 2 ** 31 - 1
    )
      throw new Error("Invalid session deadline");
    return session;
  }
  return {
    name: "sites-connector-preview",
    apply: "serve",
    config(config) {
      return {
        server: {
          host: config.server?.host ?? "127.0.0.1",
          strictPort: true,
          fs: {
            deny: [
              ".env",
              ".env.*",
              "*.{crt,pem}",
              "**/.git/**",
              "**/.sites-runtime/**",
            ],
          },
          watch: { ignored: ["**/.sites-runtime/**"] },
        },
      };
    },
    configResolved(config) {
      root = path.join(config.root, ".sites-runtime", "connector-preview");
    },
    resolveId(id) {
      if (id === moduleId) return resolvedId;
    },
    load(id, options) {
      if (id !== resolvedId) return;
      if (
        this.environment?.name === "client" ||
        (!this.environment && !options?.ssr)
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "Connected apps are only available in Site server routes.",
        );
      }
      // Auxiliary Workers load before Vite starts listening. strictPort keeps
      // this configured address stable when the server opens its socket.
      const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.config.server.port}${endpoint}`;
      const contextUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.config.server.port}${contextEndpoint}`;
      return `export default { async getContext() {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(${JSON.stringify(contextUrl)}, {
            headers: { "X-Sites-Preview": ${JSON.stringify(bridgeToken)} }
          });
          return response.json();
        } catch { return { status: "upstream_error" }; }
      }, async invoke(connectorId, actionName, args) {
        const response = await fetch(${JSON.stringify(url)}, {
          method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Sites-Preview": ${JSON.stringify(bridgeToken)} },
          body: JSON.stringify({connectorId, actionName, arguments: args})
        });
        return response.json();
      } };`;
    },
    configureServer(vite) {
      server = vite;
      vite.middlewares.use(contextEndpoint, async (req, res) => {
        res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
        res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
        if (
          req.method !== "GET" ||
          req.headers["x-sites-preview"] !== bridgeToken
        ) {
          res.statusCode = 403;
          res.end(JSON.stringify(unavailable));
          return;
        }
        try {
          const session = await readSession();
          // Preview grants do not establish hosted catalog visibility.
          if (
            !Array.isArray(session.connectorIds) ||
            !session.connectorIds.every((id) => typeof id === "string")
          )
            throw new Error("Missing selected connectors");
          res.end(
            JSON.stringify({
              status: "success",
              connectors: session.connectorIds.map((connectorId) => ({
                connectorId,
                policy: "enabled",
                tools: null,
              })),
            }),
          );
        } catch {
          res.end(JSON.stringify({ status: "binding_unavailable" }));
        }
      });
      vite.middlewares.use(endpoint, async (req, res) => {
        res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
        res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
        if (
          req.method !== "POST" ||
          req.headers["x-sites-preview"] !== bridgeToken
        ) {
          res.statusCode = 403;
          res.end(JSON.stringify(unavailable));
          return;
        }
        let requestPath;
        let responsePath;
        let submitted = false;
        try {
          const session = await readSession();
          const raw = await getRawBody(req, { limit: 65536 });
          const call = JSON.parse(
            new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(raw),
          );
          const body = JSON.stringify(call);
          const directory = path.join(root, session.id);
          const id = randomUUID();
          requestPath = path.join(directory, `${id}.request`);
          responsePath = path.join(directory, `${id}.response`);
          await writeFile(`${requestPath}.tmp`, body, { mode: 0o600 });
          await rename(`${requestPath}.tmp`, requestPath);
          submitted = true;
          // Read the owner's deadline per call, so a restarted session can use
          // longer provider waits without rebuilding or restarting the Site.
          const deadline = Math.min(
            session.expiresAt ?? Infinity,
            Date.now() + session.timeoutMs + 2000,
          );
          while (Date.now() < deadline) {
            try {
              res.end(await readFile(responsePath));
              return;
            } catch (error) {
              if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
              // Session shutdown removes the mailbox. Stop promptly even when
              // the configured provider deadline is several minutes away.
              await access(directory);
            }
            await delay(50);
          }
          res.end(JSON.stringify(uncertain));
        } catch {
          // raw-body pauses oversized requests; drain the remainder so the HTTP
          // connection can finish without retaining an unread request stream.
          req.resume();
          res.end(JSON.stringify(submitted ? uncertain : unavailable));
        } finally {
          await Promise.all(
            [requestPath, requestPath && `${requestPath}.tmp`, responsePath]
              .filter(Boolean)
              .map((file) => rm(file, { force: true })),
          );
        }
      });
    },
  };
}

// Vendored from @openai/sites-vite-plugin 0.2.0 (openai/sites#9).
// See sites-vite-plugin.LICENSE for the upstream MIT license.
import { access, cp, mkdir, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import type { Plugin } from "vite";

const localUserId = "local_seedy";
const localEmail = "seedy@sites.test";
const localFullName = "Seedy";
const localCookieName = "__sites_local_auth";
const localHosts = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"]);
const localAddresses = new Set(["127.0.0.1", "::1", "::ffff:127.0.0.1"]);
const authPaths = new Set([
  "/signin-with-chatgpt",
  "/signout-with-chatgpt",
  "/callback",
]);

async function exists(path: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await access(path);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") {
      return false;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

export function sites({ mockAuth = true } = {}): Plugin {
  let root = process.cwd();
  let command: "build" | "serve" = "build";

  return {
    name: "sites",
    configResolved(config) {
      root = config.root;
      command = config.command;
    },
    configureServer(server) {
      if (!mockAuth) return;
      const secure = Boolean(server.config.server.https);

      server.config.logger.info(`Sites local sign-in: ${localEmail}`);
      server.middlewares.use((request, response, next) => {
        for (const name of Object.keys(request.headers)) {
          if (name.startsWith("oai-authenticated-user-")) {
            removeHeader(request, name);
          }
        }

        let authority: URL;
        let url: URL;
        try {
          authority = new URL(
            `${secure ? "https" : "http"}://${request.headers.host}`,
          );
          url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", authority);
        } catch {
          if (authPaths.has((request.url ?? "/").split("?")[0])) {
            respond(response, 403);
          } else {
            next();
          }
          return;
        }

        const hostname = authority.hostname
          .replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "")
          .toLowerCase();
        if (
          !localHosts.has(hostname) ||
          !localAddresses.has(request.socket.remoteAddress ?? "") ||
          url.origin !== authority.origin
        ) {
          if (authPaths.has(url.pathname)) respond(response, 403);
          else next();
          return;
        }

        const cookies = (request.headers.cookie ?? "")
          .split(";")
          .map((cookie) => cookie.trim())
          .filter(Boolean);
        const signInCookies = cookies
          .filter((cookie) => cookie.startsWith(`${localCookieName}=`))
          .map((cookie) => cookie.slice(localCookieName.length + 1));
        const applicationCookies = cookies.filter(
          (cookie) => !cookie.startsWith(`${localCookieName}=`),
        );
        if (applicationCookies.length !== cookies.length) {
          removeHeader(request, "cookie");
          if (applicationCookies.length) {
            setHeader(request, "cookie", applicationCookies.join("; "));
          }
        }

        if (url.pathname === "/callback") {
          respond(response, 501);
          return;
        }

        const signIn = url.pathname === "/signin-with-chatgpt";
        const signOut = url.pathname === "/signout-with-chatgpt";
        if (!signIn && !signOut) {
          if (signInCookies.length === 1 && signInCookies[0] === "1") {
            setHeader(request, "oai-authenticated-user-id", localUserId);
            setHeader(request, "oai-authenticated-user-email", localEmail);
            setHeader(
              request,
              "oai-authenticated-user-full-name",
              localFullName,
            );
            setHeader(
              request,
              "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding",
              "percent-encoded-utf-8",
            );
          }
          next();
          return;
        }

        if (
          (request.headers.origin && request.headers.origin !== url.origin) ||
          request.headers["sec-fetch-site"] === "cross-site"
        ) {
          respond(response, 403);
          return;
        }

        if (
          request.headers["next-router-prefetch"] !== undefined ||
          request.headers["x-middleware-prefetch"] === "1" ||
          [request.headers.purpose, request.headers["sec-purpose"]].some(
            (value) =>
              typeof value === "string" &&
              value
                .split(/[;,]/)
                .some((part) => part.trim().toLowerCase() === "prefetch"),
          )
        ) {
          respond(response, 204);
          return;
        }

        if (
          request.method !== "GET" &&
          (!signOut || request.method !== "POST")
        ) {
          response.setHeader("Allow", signIn ? "GET" : "GET, POST");
          respond(response, 405);
          return;
        }

        response.statusCode = request.method === "POST" ? 303 : 302;
        response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
        response.setHeader(
          "Location",
          safeReturn(url.searchParams.get("return_to")),
        );
        response.setHeader(
          "Set-Cookie",
          `${localCookieName}=${signIn ? "1" : ""}; Path=/; ${
            signOut ? "Max-Age=0; " : ""
          }HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax${secure ? "; Secure" : ""}`,
        );
        response.end();
      });
    },
    async closeBundle() {
      if (command !== "build") return;

      const outputDirectory = resolve(root, "dist", ".openai");
      const hostingConfig = resolve(root, ".openai", "hosting.json");
      const drizzleSource = resolve(root, "drizzle");

      await rm(outputDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
      await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });

      await cp(hostingConfig, resolve(outputDirectory, "hosting.json"));
      if (await exists(drizzleSource)) {
        await cp(drizzleSource, resolve(outputDirectory, "drizzle"), {
          recursive: true,
        });
      }
    },
  };
}

function removeHeader(request: IncomingMessage, name: string): void {
  delete request.headers[name];
  for (let index = request.rawHeaders.length - 2; index >= 0; index -= 2) {
    if (request.rawHeaders[index]?.toLowerCase() === name) {
      request.rawHeaders.splice(index, 2);
    }
  }
}

function setHeader(
  request: IncomingMessage,
  name: string,
  value: string,
): void {
  removeHeader(request, name);
  request.headers[name] = value;
  request.rawHeaders.push(name, value);
}

function respond(response: ServerResponse, status: number): void {
  response.statusCode = status;
  response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
  response.end();
}

function safeReturn(value: string | null): string {
  if (!value?.startsWith("/") || value.startsWith("//")) return "/";

  try {
    const url = new URL(value, "http://localhost");
    if (url.origin !== "http://localhost" || authPaths.has(url.pathname)) {
      return "/";
    }
    return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}

import handler from "vinext/server/fetch-handler";
import { runWithConnectorBinding } from "../lib/connector-context";
import type { ConnectorBinding } from "../lib/connector-contract.mjs";

export default {
  fetch(request: Request, env: Cloudflare.Env, ctx: ExecutionContext<{ CONNECTORS?: ConnectorBinding }>) {
    let binding = ctx.props?.CONNECTORS;
    // Local preview emulates the same request-scoped capability. This branch and
    // the auxiliary service binding are absent from production builds.
    if (import.meta.env.DEV && !binding && env.CONNECTORS) {
      const preview = env.CONNECTORS;
      const expiresAt = Date.now() + 60_000;
      binding = {
        async getContext() {
          if (Date.now() >= expiresAt) return { status: "request_context_expired" };
          return preview.getContext?.() ?? { status: "binding_unavailable" };
        },
        async invoke(connectorId, actionName, args) {
          if (Date.now() >= expiresAt) {
            return { status: "request_context_expired", message: "This request has expired. Please try again." };
          }
          return preview.invoke(connectorId, actionName, args);
        },
      };
    }
    return runWithConnectorBinding(binding, () => handler.fetch(request, env, ctx));
  },
};

type InstallEvent = Event & { prompt(): Promise<void>; userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: string }> };
export function WorkspaceHeader({ onMenu, onNew, onNotice, showNavigation }: { onMenu(): void; onNew(): void; onNotice(message: string): void; showNavigation: boolean }) {
  const install = useRef<InstallEvent | null>(null);
  const [printClean, setPrintClean] = useState(true);
  const [installHelp, setInstallHelp] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const receive = (event: Event) => { event.preventDefault(); install.current = event as InstallEvent; };
    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', receive);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', receive);
  }, []);
  async function installApp() {
    if (!install.current) { setInstallHelp(true); return; }
    try { await install.current.prompt(); const choice = await install.current.userChoice; if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') onNotice('Prism 앱 설치를 시작했습니다.'); }
    catch { onNotice('브라우저 메뉴에서 앱 설치를 선택해주세요.'); }
    finally { install.current = null; }
  }

export function NotebookDialog({ open, onOpenChange, onCreate }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void; onCreate(file: File): Promise<void> }) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [content, setContent] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  async function save(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault(); if (!title.trim() || saving) return;
    setSaving(true); setError('');
    try {
      const name = title.trim().replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '-').slice(0, 100);
      await onCreate(new File([`# ${title.trim()}\n\n${content}`], `${name}.md`, { type: 'text/markdown' }));
      setTitle(''); setContent(''); onOpenChange(false);
    } catch (failure) { setError(failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : '노트북을 저장하지 못했습니다.'); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  }
  return <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={value => { if (!saving) onOpenChange(value); }}><Dialog.Portal><Dialog.Overlay className="prism-dialog-overlay"/><Dialog.Content className="prism-dialog-content prism-notebook-dialog"><form onSubmit={save}><div className="prism-dialog-heading"><Dialog.Title>새 노트북</Dialog.Title><Dialog.Close className="prism-icon" aria-label="닫기" disabled={saving}><X size={20}/></Dialog.Close></div><Dialog.Description>노트를 문서로 저장하고 채팅에 첨부해 활용하세요.</Dialog.Description><label>이름<input disabled={saving} autoFocus required maxLength={100} value={title} onChange={event => setTitle(event.target.value)} placeholder="노트북 이름"/></label><label>노트<textarea disabled={saving} rows={8} maxLength={50000} value={content} onChange={event => setContent(event.target.value)} placeholder="아이디어나 참고할 내용을 적어주세요"/></label>{error && <p role="alert" className="prism-form-error">{error}</p>}<div className="prism-dialog-actions"><Dialog.Close className="prism-secondary-button" disabled={saving}>취소</Dialog.Close><button className="prism-primary-button" type="submit" disabled={saving || !title.trim()}>{saving && <LoaderCircle size={16} className="spin"/>}만들기</button></div></form></Dialog.Content></Dialog.Portal></Dialog.Root>;
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { DropdownMenu, Popover } from 'radix-ui';
import { ArrowUp, Check, ChevronDown, FileText, FolderOpen, Image, LoaderCircle, Mic, Plus, Search, SlidersHorizontal, Square, Upload, Video, X, ScrollText, PlugZap } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Model, Provider } from '../../lib/models';

type FileItem = { id: string; name: string };
type Recognition = {
  lang: string; continuous: boolean; interimResults: boolean;
  onresult: ((event: { results: ArrayLike<ArrayLike<{ transcript: string }>> }) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((event: { error: string }) => void) | null;
  onend: (() => void) | null;
  start(): void; stop(): void; abort(): void;

type SpeechWindow = Window & {

export type ComposerProps = {
  prompt: string; onPrompt(value: string): void;

export function Composer(props: ComposerProps) {
  const [modelOpen, setModelOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
  const recognition = useRef<Recognition | null>(null);
  const latestPrompt = useRef(props.prompt);
  latestPrompt.current = props.prompt;
  const mentionRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<Array<{id:string;name:string}>>([]);
  const [skillError, setSkillError] = useState('');
  const [loadingSkills, setLoadingSkills] = useState(false);
  const mention = /(?:^|\s)@([^\s@]*)$/.exec(props.prompt);
  const mentionOpen = !!mention;
  const { inputRef, prompt } = props;

  useEffect(() => {
    const input = inputRef.current;
    if (!input) return;
    input.style.height = '24px';
    input.style.height = `${Math.min(144, input.scrollHeight)}px`;
  }, [prompt, inputRef]);
  useEffect(() => () => recognition.current?.abort(), []);
  useEffect(() => { if (props.busy) recognition.current?.abort(); }, [props.busy]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mentionOpen || !props.signedIn) return;
    const controller = new AbortController(); setLoadingSkills(true); setSkillError('');
    fetch('/api/settings', {signal:controller.signal}).then(async response => {
      const data = await response.json() as {error?:string;skills?:Array<{id:string;name:string}>};
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || '스킬을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) setSkills(Array.isArray(data.skills) ? data.skills : []);
    }).catch(error => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setSkillError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '스킬을 불러오지 못했습니다.'); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoadingSkills(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [mentionOpen, props.signedIn]);

  function dictate() {
    if (listening) { recognition.current?.stop(); return; }
    const browser = window as SpeechWindow;
    const API = browser.SpeechRecognition ?? browser.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!API) { props.onError('이 브라우저에서는 음성 입력을 지원하지 않습니다. Chrome에서 사용하거나 직접 입력해주세요.'); return; }
    const speech = new API();
    speech.lang = navigator.language || 'ko-KR';
    speech.continuous = false;
    speech.interimResults = false;
    speech.onresult = event => {
      const transcript = Array.from(event.results).map(result => result[0]?.transcript || '').join(' ');
      props.onPrompt([latestPrompt.current, transcript].filter(Boolean).join(' ').slice(0, 32000));
      inputRef.current?.focus();
    };
    speech.onerror = event => {
      if (event.error !== 'aborted') props.onError(event.error === 'not-allowed' ? '음성 입력을 사용하려면 브라우저의 마이크 권한을 허용해주세요.' : '음성을 인식하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해주세요.');
      setListening(false);
    };
    speech.onend = () => setListening(false);
    recognition.current = speech;
    try { speech.start(); setListening(true); }
    catch { props.onError('마이크를 시작할 수 없습니다. 브라우저 권한을 확인해주세요.'); }
  }

  const filtered = props.models.filter(model => `${model.name} ${model.provider}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));
  return <div className="gemini-compose-wrap">
    {props.selectedSkill && <div className="gemini-attachments"><span><ScrollText size={14}/><span>{props.selectedSkill.name}</span><button type="button" disabled={props.busy} aria-label="스킬 적용 해제" onClick={() => props.onSkill(null)}><X size={14}/></button></span></div>}
    {props.attached.length > 0 && <div className="gemini-attachments" aria-label="첨부 파일">
      {props.attached.map(id => <span key={id}><FileText size={14}/><span>{props.files.find(file => file.id === id)?.name || '첨부 문서'}</span><button type="button" disabled={props.busy} aria-label="첨부 제거" onClick={() => props.onDetach(id)}><X size={14}/></button></span>)}
    </div>}
    <div className="gemini-composer" data-multiline={prompt.includes('\n') || prompt.length > 100}>
      <DropdownMenu.Root>
        <DropdownMenu.Trigger asChild><button type="button" className="gemini-add" aria-label="파일 및 도구 추가" disabled={props.busy || props.uploading}>{props.uploading ? <LoaderCircle size={24} className="spin"/> : <Plus size={26}/>}</button></DropdownMenu.Trigger>
        <DropdownMenu.Portal><DropdownMenu.Content className="prism-tool-menu" side="bottom" align="start" sideOffset={12} collisionPadding={12}>
          <DropdownMenu.Item className="prism-tool-item" onSelect={props.onUpload}><Upload size={18}/>파일 업로드</DropdownMenu.Item>
          <DropdownMenu.Item className="prism-tool-item" onSelect={() => props.onNavigate('/files')}><FolderOpen size={18}/>내 파일 열기</DropdownMenu.Item>
          {props.files.length > 0 && <DropdownMenu.Sub><DropdownMenu.SubTrigger className="prism-tool-item"><FileText size={18}/>저장된 문서 첨부<ChevronDown size={14}/></DropdownMenu.SubTrigger><DropdownMenu.Portal><DropdownMenu.SubContent className="prism-tool-menu" sideOffset={4} collisionPadding={12}>{props.files.map(file => <DropdownMenu.CheckboxItem key={file.id} className="prism-tool-item" checked={props.attached.includes(file.id)} onCheckedChange={checked => checked ? props.onAttach(file.id) : props.onDetach(file.id)}><FileText size={16}/><span>{file.name}</span><DropdownMenu.ItemIndicator><Check size={15}/></DropdownMenu.ItemIndicator></DropdownMenu.CheckboxItem>)}</DropdownMenu.SubContent></DropdownMenu.Portal></DropdownMenu.Sub>}
          <DropdownMenu.Separator className="prism-menu-divider"/>
          <DropdownMenu.Item className="prism-tool-item" onSelect={() => props.onNavigate('/images')}><Image size={18}/>이미지 생성<span className="prism-unavailable-label">미연결</span></DropdownMenu.Item>
          <DropdownMenu.Item className="prism-tool-item" onSelect={() => props.onNavigate('/videos')}><Video size={18}/>동영상 생성<span className="prism-unavailable-label">미연결</span></DropdownMenu.Item>
        </DropdownMenu.Content></DropdownMenu.Portal>
      </DropdownMenu.Root>
      <textarea ref={inputRef} className="gemini-prompt" aria-label="메시지 입력" value={prompt} rows={1} maxLength={32000} disabled={props.busy}
        placeholder={props.compare ? '여러 모델에 같은 질문을 입력하세요' : props.mode === 'work' ? '어떤 작업을 도와드릴까요?' : '커넥터, 스킬 등을 보려면 @ 입력하세요'}
        onChange={event => props.onPrompt(event.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={event => { if (mentionOpen && event.key === 'ArrowDown') { event.preventDefault(); mentionRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button')?.focus(); return; } if (event.key === 'Escape' && mentionOpen) { event.preventDefault(); props.onPrompt(prompt.replace(/(^|\s)@[^\s@]*$/, '$1')); return; } if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing && event.keyCode !== 229) { event.preventDefault(); if (mentionOpen) mentionRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button')?.focus(); else props.onSend(); } }}/>
      <div className="gemini-composer-actions">
        {!props.compare && <Popover.Root open={modelOpen} onOpenChange={open => { setModelOpen(open); if (!open) setQuery(''); }}>
          <Popover.Trigger asChild><button type="button" className="gemini-model-trigger" disabled={props.busy} aria-label={`모델 선택: ${props.selected.name}`}><span>{props.selected.name}</span><ChevronDown size={18}/></button></Popover.Trigger>
          <Popover.Portal><Popover.Content className="prism-model-menu" side="bottom" align="end" sideOffset={14} collisionPadding={12}>
            <div className="prism-model-menu-heading"><strong>모델 선택</strong><Popover.Close className="prism-icon" aria-label="모델 선택 닫기"><X size={18}/></Popover.Close></div>
            <label className="prism-model-search"><Search size={17}/><input placeholder="모델 검색" aria-label="모델 검색" value={query} onChange={event => setQuery(event.target.value)}/></label>
            <div className="prism-model-options" role="group" aria-label="AI 모델">
              {filtered.map(model => <button type="button" key={model.id} aria-pressed={model.id === props.selected.id} className="prism-model-option" onClick={() => { props.onModel(model); setModelOpen(false); setQuery(''); }}><span className={`prism-provider-dot ${model.provider}`}/><span><strong>{model.name}</strong><small>{model.category} · {props.providers[model.provider].configured ? '연결됨' : 'API 연결 필요'}</small></span>{model.id === props.selected.id && <Check size={18}/>}</button>)}
              {filtered.length === 0 && <p className="prism-menu-empty">일치하는 모델이 없습니다.</p>}
            </div>
            {props.selected.efforts.length > 0 && <label className="prism-reasoning"><SlidersHorizontal size={17}/><span>추론 수준</span><select aria-label="추론 수준" value={props.effort} onChange={event => props.onEffort(event.target.value)}>{props.selected.efforts.map(effort => <option key={effort} value={effort}>{effort}</option>)}</select></label>}
            <button type="button" className="prism-model-library" onClick={() => { setModelOpen(false); props.onNavigate('/models'); }}>전체 모델 라이브러리</button>
          </Popover.Content></Popover.Portal>
        </Popover.Root>}
        {props.compare && <label className="prism-compare-effort"><SlidersHorizontal size={16}/><select aria-label="비교 추론 수준" disabled={props.busy} value={props.effort} onChange={event => props.onEffort(event.target.value)}>{['low','medium','high'].map(value => <option key={value} value={value}>{value}</option>)}</select></label>}
        <button type="button" className={'gemini-mic' + (listening ? ' is-listening' : '')} aria-label={listening ? '음성 입력 중지' : '음성 입력'} title="브라우저 음성 인식 · 마이크 사용 시에만 시작" aria-pressed={listening} disabled={props.busy} onClick={dictate}><Mic size={22}/></button>
        {props.busy ? <button type="button" className="gemini-send" aria-label="응답 중지" onClick={props.onStop}><Square size={16} fill="currentColor"/></button> : prompt.trim() ? <button type="button" className="gemini-send" aria-label="메시지 보내기" disabled={!props.ready || props.uploading} onClick={props.onSend}><ArrowUp size={21}/></button> : null}
      </div>
    </div>
    {mentionOpen && <div ref={mentionRef} className="prism-mention-menu prism-tool-menu" role="dialog" aria-label="스킬 및 연결" onKeyDown={event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); props.onPrompt(prompt.replace(/(^|\s)@[^\s@]*$/, '$1')); inputRef.current?.focus(); } }}>
      {loadingSkills && <p className="prism-tool-note" role="status">스킬을 불러오는 중…</p>}
      {skillError && <p className="prism-tool-note" role="alert">{skillError}</p>}
      {skills.filter(skill => skill.name.toLowerCase().includes((mention?.[1] || '').toLowerCase())).map(skill => <button type="button" key={skill.id} className="prism-tool-item" onClick={() => { props.onSkill(skill); props.onPrompt(prompt.replace(/(^|\s)@[^\s@]*$/, '$1')); inputRef.current?.focus(); }}><ScrollText size={18}/><span>{skill.name}</span></button>)}
      <button type="button" className="prism-tool-item" onClick={() => props.onNavigate('/customize/skills')}><ScrollText size={18}/>스킬 관리</button>
      <button type="button" className="prism-tool-item" onClick={() => props.onNavigate('/settings')}><PlugZap size={18}/>AI 연결 설정</button>
    </div>}
    {listening && <p className="prism-listening" role="status">듣고 있습니다… 음성 인식은 브라우저의 음성 서비스를 사용할 수 있습니다.</p>}
  </div>;
}

import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/d1";
import * as schema from "./schema";

export function getDb() {
  if (!env.DB) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cloudflare D1 binding `DB` is unavailable. Set the `d1` field in .openai/hosting.json to `DB` or let your control plane inject the real binding values before using the database."
    );
  }

  return drizzle(env.DB, { schema });
}

# Gemini-reference interface implementation

## Scope and platform

This change applies the two supplied Gemini screenshots to **Open Prism**, preserving its authentication, provider adapters, storage, quota enforcement, and model availability rules. Styling is an independent reproduction of the supplied references. Gemini's private backend and application source are not used.

| Project | Configuration evidence | Runtime |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `JackRo682/open-prism` | `.openai/hosting.json`, `vite.config.ts`, `app/chatgpt-auth.ts`, `lib/provider.ts` | ChatGPT Sites; Cloudflare Workers; D1 `DB`; R2 `BUCKET`; Sign in with ChatGPT. Sites project: `appgprj_6ad1c380cba88191a9a68b9138409d20`. |
| `JackRo682/SignalBrief` | `apps/web/vercel.json`, `apps/web/package.json`, `apps/web/src/lib/supabase.ts`, `supabase/hosted/README.md` in that separate repository | Vercel and Supabase. No changes to that project are included here. |

GitHub discovery found nine accessible repositories under `JackRo682`; `open-prism` contained this multi-model chat application. Its remote branch inventory contained `main` at inspection time. No Supabase or Vercel migration is required for this redesign.

## Visual reference

The attached home PNG is **1440 × 703**; the settings PNG is **1440 × 699**. The accompanying inspection notes describe a separate 1440 × 709 browser session.

| Element | Reference measurement or treatment |
| --- | --- |
| Sidebar | 288px wide; `#1f1f1f`; icon centers approximately x26, labels starting at x48, and 32px main-navigation row pitch. |
| Workspace | Starts at x288. Base `#0f0f0f` with a broad blue-violet glow reproduced with layered radial gradients. |
| Heading | Workspace center x864; approximately 36px regular type with 44px line height. Home glyph bounds approximately x687–1032, y238–271. |
| Composer | x534, y321, width660, height64 in the home PNG; y320 in the settings PNG. `#1e1f20`, radius32, no border. |
| Centering | `288 + (1152 - 660) / 2 = 534`; centered inside the workspace. |
| Composer layout | `36px minmax(0, 1fr) auto` grid, 12px padding, 17px/24px prompt typography. Longer model labels and extra controls use the available space. |
| Composer shadow | `0 2px 8px -2px rgb(0 0 0 / 16%)`; 100ms transition. |
| Chat/Work control | 14px below the composer; selected pill approximately 56 × 38px with `#343537` fill. |
| Settings menu | x244, y0, width240, height664 in the settings PNG; radius20, padding8, `#1f1f1f`. |
| Menu rows | 224 × 36px, 14px/20px text. Fifteen rows plus three 36px headings and 16px outer padding produce the 664px natural height. |
| Menu effects | `0 0 20px rgb(0 0 0 / 28%)`; 120ms entrance with `cubic-bezier(0, 0, .2, 1)`; 8px submenu overlap. |

Original animation keyframes and a mobile reference were not supplied. The opacity/scale entrance, reduced-motion behavior, collapsed sidebar, and mobile drawer are implementation choices. Menus use collision handling and internal scrolling rather than fixed screenshot coordinates. Typography uses local fallbacks and does not bundle proprietary Google font files.

## Source organization

| Source | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| `app/workspace.tsx` | Shared navigation, route composition, chat state, streaming lifecycle, uploads, notebooks, and sharing. |
| `app/reference-shell.css` | Reference layout, colors, glow, composer, navigation, and responsive shell. |
| `app/workspace-details.css` | Supporting pages, tool/model menus, dialogs, and printing. |
| `components/workspace/composer.tsx` | Autosizing prompt, Korean IME-safe submission, model/reasoning selection, attachments, speech recognition, stop/send, and `@` skills. |
| `components/workspace/chrome.tsx` | Prism mark, browser print/install actions, and Markdown notebook creation. |
| `components/workspace/settings-menu.tsx` | Radix settings menu with Theme, Help, and Location submenus. |
| `components/workspace/theme.tsx` | Theme persistence, system changes, and cross-tab updates. |
| `components/workspace/chat-search.tsx` | Debounced search, cancellation, keyboard navigation, and focus restoration. |
| `components/workspace/settings-pages.tsx`, `settings.css` | Settings forms, skills, memory import, sharing, feedback, capability states, and portal styling. |
| `lib/workspace-settings.ts` | Bounded validation, atomic patches, request-time context, and attachment omission in public snapshots. |
| `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` | Existing API plus settings, search, and public shares. |
| `drizzle/0003_workspace_settings_and_shares.sql` | `workspace_settings`, `public_shares`, indexes, and the conversation relationship. |

## Routes and actual behavior

| Route or action | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| `/`, `/chat`, `/chat/:id` | Welcome or saved conversation; provider requests follow existing server policy. |
| `/library`, `/history` | Existing saved-conversation library. |
| `/models`, `/compare` | Existing catalog/comparison; beta restricts models and disables comparison. |
| `/files`, new notebook | Private text files; a notebook is a real Markdown file saved through `/api/files`. |
| Search / Ctrl+K / Cmd+K | Searches the owner's titles and message text through `/api/search`. |
| `/personalization-settings` | Saves personalization consent, instructions, and editable memory. |
| `/customize/skills` | Creates, edits, and deletes saved instructions; `@` selects a skill for a request. |
| `/gems/view` | Directs users to saved skills. |
| `/gemini-spark` | Persists plan-first or results-first Work preferences, applied to provider context. |
| `/browser-settings` | Shows the current browser and unconnected remote-browser capability. |
| `/import` | Copies a memory-export prompt, previews pasted text, and replaces memory after confirmation. Chat archive import is unavailable. |
| `/sharing`, `/share/:token` | Owner-managed public snapshots and their public reader. |
| `/usage`, `/billing`, `/settings` | Existing actual usage, account, provider, and beta/billing information. |
| `/feedback` | Prefills a GitHub issue for user review and submission. |
| `/images`, `/videos`, `/scheduled` | Explicit unavailable states; no media provider or scheduler is connected. |
| Microphone | Browser speech recognition when supported; requires the microphone action and browser permission. |
| Save as PDF | Browser printing; the user chooses a PDF destination. |
| App install | Available browser install prompt or installation guidance. |

Google Drive OAuth, remote browser automation, remote code execution, image/video generation, background delivery, and scheduling have no active integrations. Work mode supplies response instructions and does not execute external tasks. The Location submenu can request device coordinates for display; it does not transmit them to an AI provider.

## Backend contracts

Private endpoints use the authenticated Sites application user ID. Existing mutation-origin checks remain in place.

| Endpoint | Contract |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /api/settings` | Owner's flat settings object or defaults. |
| `PATCH /api/settings` | Validates known bounded fields and atomically patches changed fields in D1. Its separate 600 KB request budget accommodates the maximum settings payload. |
| `GET /api/search?q=...` | `{ results }`; title/body search with escaped SQL wildcards and owner scope. At most 50 results, queries up to 200 characters; blank queries return no results. |
| `GET /api/shares` | `{ shares }` for the owner. |
| `POST /api/shares` | `{ conversationId }` creates an owner-checked snapshot; returns `{ id, token, url }`. |
| `DELETE /api/shares/:id` | Owner-checked revocation. |
| `GET /api/public-shares/:token` | Public `{ title, created_at, messages }`; invalid/revoked tokens return 404. |
| `POST /api/chat` | Existing fields plus `workspaceMode: 'chat'` or `'work'` and optional `skillId`. Resolves current settings and the owned skill on every request. |

Settings fields: `personalizationEnabled` (default false), `customInstructions` (up to 4,000 characters), `memory` (up to 8,000), `workResponseStyle` (`plan-first` or `direct`), and `skills` (up to 20; each name up to 60 characters and instructions up to 4,000).

Personalization includes memory/instructions only while enabled. An explicitly selected skill applies independently. Preferences are added to the provider request without insertion into stored user messages. Plan-first and results-first Work choices both affect provider context and grant no external-action permissions.

Public tokens contain 32 cryptographically random bytes encoded as 64 hexadecimal characters. Snapshots are limited to 1 MB and 100 links per owner, including concurrent creation. Attached-document suffixes are omitted; ordinary conversation text, including any quotes in responses, remains visible. Revocation is checked on each read with `no-store` responses.

## Configuration, migration, and verification

The [README](../README.md#runtime-configuration) lists required existing bindings and variables. This UI adds no provider secret or OAuth client. Provider accounts still need model access and quota. Keep `PAYMENTS_MODE=disabled` for the free beta; this change enables no subscription or paid checkout.

Apply all D1 migrations, including `0003_workspace_settings_and_shares.sql`, before using the new endpoints. Preserve the existing Sites project ID and `DB`/`BUCKET` bindings.

```sh
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit
node --test tests/integration.mjs
pnpm build
```

The **36 integration tests** use isolated SQLite and mocked provider transports. New coverage includes bounded owner-isolated settings, fresh preference/skill application, message-text search and wildcard escaping, public-snapshot ownership/redaction/revocation, the settings-only body-size budget, and concurrent public-link limits. Existing authentication, streaming persistence, uploads, quotas, and accounting checks are retained.

Use `pnpm dev` in a portable checkout. In managed Sites, use `sites-preview start <absolute-checkout>` and its exact returned URL. Build/package hosted releases through the Sites workflow from the pushed source commit. Live provider access and visual/interaction behavior require separate acceptance checks; automated transport tests use no live API credentials.

# Redesign verification — 4 November 2026

## Automated checks

- TypeScript: `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit --pretty false` passed.
- API integration suite: 36/36 tests passed using an isolated SQLite database and mocked provider transports.
- New checks cover settings ownership and limits, fresh model context, disabled personalization, saved skills, literal title/body search, immutable public snapshots, attachment-body omission, revocation, the settings body budget, and concurrent public-link limits.
- `git diff --check` passed.

## Cloud-browser checks

| Check | Observed result |
| --- | --- |
| Desktop viewport | Real application rendered at 1440 × 703 CSS pixels in a responsive preview frame. |
| Sidebar and composer | Sidebar 288px; composer 660 × 64px, left edge x534, centered in the workspace. |
| Settings menu | x244, y0, 240 × 664px; background `rgb(31,31,31)`; entrance duration 0.12s. |
| Theme | Light changed the root theme and page surface; Dark restored the reference appearance. |
| Draft | Typing reveals Send while retaining the microphone; signed-out send returns the real authentication error. |
| Chat search | Opens with the search field focused and an accurate signed-out state; Escape closes it. |
| Menus | Model catalog, reasoning selection, upload/tools, Theme, and settings routes render. |
| Secondary route | Personalization opens `/personalization-settings`; anonymous users see sign-in rather than editable account data. |
| Unconnected media | Image action opens a clear unavailable state with working return/settings navigation. |
| Mobile viewport | 390 × 844; document width remains 390px with no horizontal overflow. |
| Mobile focus | Closed sidebar is hidden; opening traps focus and makes workspace inert. Closing an open menu with Escape leaves the drawer open. New chat closes the drawer. Resizing to desktop removes the inert state. |
| Console | No application-origin error or warning entries were observed in the inspected console log window. Browser-extension errors are unrelated to the application. |

The screenshots below show the 1440 × 703 layout scaled to the cloud browser's available width (1363 × 665 image pixels). The verification toolbar is cropped out. They show an anonymous development session, not a fabricated signed-in account.

No live model request, microphone/location permission grant, new public share of user data, payment, or authentication flow was performed during visual QA. This record does not claim pixel equality for proprietary fonts, unprovided mobile references, or unseen Gemini screens.

UTF8_BYTES: 1499
SHA256: 4a5ceb328d4f6d8782be707953dc3b39970a4f851cd250ee1e72c32e9d219147
CONTENT:
import { BillingError } from './billing';

// Micro-USD, reserved before provider access in one atomic SQLite statement.
// Failed/uncertain requests retain their reservation; deleting chats cannot reset it.
export const BETA_OUTPUT = 1024;
export const BETA_INPUT_BYTES = 16000;
export function betaCost(input: number, output: number) {
  return input * 2 + output * 10;
}
export async function reserveBeta(sql: D1Database, owner: string, id: string, input: number, now: number) {
  const amount = betaCost(input, BETA_OUTPUT);
  const day = Math.floor(now / 86400000) * 86400000;
  const row = await sql.prepare(`INSERT INTO usage
    (id,user_id,model,provider,status,created_at,rate_snapshot,reserved_amount)
    SELECT ?,?,'gpt-6.1-sol','openai','pending',?,'beta:v1',?
    WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM usage WHERE user_id=? AND rate_snapshot='beta:v1' AND created_at>=?) < 20
    AND (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(reserved_amount),0) FROM usage WHERE user_id=? AND rate_snapshot='beta:v1' AND created_at>=?) + ? <= 200000
    AND (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(reserved_amount),0) FROM usage WHERE rate_snapshot='beta:v1') + ? <= 8000000
    AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM usage WHERE user_id=? AND rate_snapshot='beta:v1' AND status='pending') < 2
    RETURNING id`).bind(id, owner, now, amount, owner, day, owner, day, amount, amount, owner).first();
  if (!row) throw new BillingError('Beta allowance reached or two responses are already running. Try again later. No payment is required.', 429);
  return amount;
}

===== BEGIN FILE: lib/billing.ts =====
UTF8_BYTES: 12899
SHA256: e3a78734f7751cea3c092d392a2088b7f92c98676a77af151b6db5072051506f
CONTENT:
import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { MODELS } from './models';

// One unit is 1/1,000,000 KRW. All ledger arithmetic uses safe integers.
export const WON = 1_000_000;
export const TOP_UPS = [10_000, 30_000, 50_000];
export type Rates = { input: number; output: number };
export type BillingConfig = {
  rates: Record<string, Rates>;
  refundPolicy: string;
  validityPolicy: string;
  registrationNumber: string;
  address: string;
  supportEmail: string;
};
export const emptyConfig: BillingConfig = { rates: {}, refundPolicy: '', validityPolicy: '', registrationNumber: '', address: '', supportEmail: '' };
export class BillingError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, public status = 400) { super(message); }
}
export function validateConfig(value: unknown): BillingConfig {
  const v = value as BillingConfig;
  if (!v || typeof v !== 'object' || !v.rates || typeof v.rates !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v.rates)) throw new BillingError('모델별 단가를 입력하세요.');
  const rates: Record<string, Rates> = {};
  for (const [id, rate] of Object.entries(v.rates)) {
    if (!MODELS.some(m => m.id === id) || !rate || !['input', 'output'].every(k => Number.isSafeInteger(rate[k as keyof Rates]) && rate[k as keyof Rates] > 0 && rate[k as keyof Rates] <= 1_000_000)) throw new BillingError('단가는 100만 토큰당 원화 정수(1~1,000,000원)로 입력하세요.');
    rates[id] = { input: rate.input, output: rate.output };
  }
  const result = { rates } as BillingConfig;
  for (const key of ['refundPolicy', 'validityPolicy', 'registrationNumber', 'address', 'supportEmail'] as const) {
    if (typeof v[key] !== 'string' || v[key].length > 4000) throw new BillingError('출시 설정 값이 올바르지 않습니다.');
    result[key] = v[key].trim();
  }
  if (result.supportEmail && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(result.supportEmail)) throw new BillingError('고객지원 이메일을 확인하세요.');
  return result;
}
export async function getBillingConfig(sql: D1Database): Promise<BillingConfig> {
  const row = await sql.prepare("SELECT value FROM billing_config WHERE key='launch'").first<{value:string}>();
  return row ? validateConfig(JSON.parse(row.value)) : { ...emptyConfig, rates: {} };
}
export function launchBlockers(config: BillingConfig): string[] {
  const blockers: string[] = [];
  if (!Object.keys(config.rates).length) blockers.push('모델별 차감 단가 미확정');
  if (!config.refundPolicy) blockers.push('환불 기준 미확정');
  if (!config.validityPolicy) blockers.push('충전금 유효기간 미확정');
  if (!config.registrationNumber) blockers.push('사업자등록번호 미등록');
  if (!config.address) blockers.push('사업장 주소 미등록');
  if (!config.supportEmail) blockers.push('고객지원 이메일 미등록');
  return blockers;
}
export function paymentMode(config: BillingConfig): 'disabled'|'test'|'live' {
  const mode = env.PAYMENTS_MODE;
  if (mode === 'test' && /^test_(g)?sk_/.test(env.TOSS_SECRET_KEY || '') && /^test_(g)?ck_/.test(env.TOSS_CLIENT_KEY || '')) return 'test';
  if (mode === 'live' && env.LAUNCH_APPROVED === 'true' && !launchBlockers(config).length && /^live_(g)?sk_/.test(env.TOSS_SECRET_KEY || '') && /^live_(g)?ck_/.test(env.TOSS_CLIENT_KEY || '')) return 'live';
  return 'disabled';
}
export async function balance(sql: D1Database, owner: string, mode = 'live'): Promise<number> {
  const row = await sql.prepare('SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount),0) AS balance FROM wallet_events WHERE user_id=? AND mode=?').bind(owner, mode).first<{balance:number}>();
  return row?.balance || 0;
}
export async function reserve(sql: D1Database, owner: string, requestId: string, amount: number) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount <= 0) throw new BillingError('요청 비용을 계산할 수 없습니다.');
  // The balance predicate and debit run as one SQLite write statement, across
  // all conversations and Workers. No read-then-update race can overspend.
  const row = await sql.prepare(`INSERT INTO wallet_events (id,user_id,mode,kind,amount,reference,created_at)
    SELECT ?,?,'live','reserve',?,?,? WHERE
    (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount),0) FROM wallet_events WHERE user_id=? AND mode='live') >= ?
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM account_flags WHERE user_id=?)
    AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM wallet_events r WHERE r.user_id=? AND r.kind='reserve'
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM wallet_events s WHERE s.reference='settle:' || substr(r.reference,9))) < 3
    RETURNING id`).bind(crypto.randomUUID(),owner,-amount,'reserve:'+requestId,Date.now(),owner,amount,owner,owner).first();
  if (!row) throw new BillingError('사용 가능 잔액이 부족하거나 정산 대기 요청이 3개 있습니다. 충전 내역을 확인하세요.', 402);
}
export async function settle(sql: D1Database, owner: string, requestId: string, reserved: number, charge: number) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(charge) || charge < 0 || charge > reserved) throw new BillingError('사용량 확인이 필요해 예약 잔액을 보류했습니다.', 503);
  await sql.prepare(`INSERT INTO wallet_events (id,user_id,mode,kind,amount,reference,created_at)
    SELECT ?,?,'live','settle',?,?,? WHERE EXISTS
    (SELECT 1 FROM wallet_events WHERE user_id=? AND reference=? AND amount=?)
    ON CONFLICT(reference) DO NOTHING`).bind(crypto.randomUUID(),owner,reserved-charge,'settle:'+requestId,Date.now(),owner,'reserve:'+requestId,-reserved).run();
  const applied=await sql.prepare('SELECT amount FROM wallet_events WHERE user_id=? AND reference=?').bind(owner,'settle:'+requestId).first<{amount:number}>();
  if(applied&&applied.amount!==reserved-charge)throw new BillingError('이미 다른 금액으로 정산된 요청입니다.',409);
}
export function cost(rate: Rates, input: number, output: number) {
  if (![input,output].every(n => Number.isSafeInteger(n) && n >= 0)) throw new BillingError('제공사 사용량을 검증하지 못했습니다.',503);
  const result = input * rate.input + output * rate.output;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(result)) throw new BillingError('사용량이 계산 범위를 초과했습니다.',503);
  return result;
}
export async function billingOverview(sql: D1Database, owner: string) {
  const config = await getBillingConfig(sql), mode = paymentMode(config);
  return { business: {name:'종합상사 루비',representative:'노휘석',registrationNumber:config.registrationNumber,address:config.address,supportEmail:config.supportEmail},
    topUps:TOP_UPS,mode,rates:config.rates,refundPolicy:config.refundPolicy,validityPolicy:config.validityPolicy,
    blockers:launchBlockers(config),balance:await balance(sql,owner),testBalance:await balance(sql,owner,'test'),
    events:(await sql.prepare('SELECT kind,amount,mode,reference,created_at FROM wallet_events WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100').bind(owner).all()).results,
    payments:(await sql.prepare('SELECT id,amount,mode,status,created_at FROM payments WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50').bind(owner).all()).results };
}
async function toss(path: string, method = 'GET', body?: unknown, idempotency?: string) {
  const response = await fetch('https://api.tosspayments.com/v1/payments'+path,{method,
    headers:{Authorization:'Basic '+btoa(env.TOSS_SECRET_KEY+':'),'Content-Type':'application/json',...(idempotency?{'Idempotency-Key':idempotency}:{})},
    ...(body?{body:JSON.stringify(body)}:{}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
  if (!response.ok) throw new BillingError('결제 제공사 확인에 실패했습니다. 같은 주문으로 다시 시도해주세요.',502);
  return response.json() as Promise<any>;
}
export async function creditPayment(sql: D1Database, payment: any) {
  const order = await sql.prepare('SELECT * FROM payments WHERE id=?').bind(payment.orderId).first<any>();
  if (!order || payment.status!=='DONE' || payment.currency!=='KRW' || payment.totalAmount!==order.amount || payment.balanceAmount!==order.amount || payment.paymentKey!==order.payment_key || (payment.cancels?.length || 0)>0) throw new BillingError('결제 금액 또는 상태가 주문과 일치하지 않습니다.',409);
  // Unique reference + atomic batch: callbacks and retries never credit twice.
  await sql.batch([
    sql.prepare("INSERT INTO wallet_events (id,user_id,mode,kind,amount,reference,created_at) SELECT ?,user_id,mode,'topup',amount*1000000,?,? FROM payments WHERE id=? AND status IN ('pending','paid') ON CONFLICT(reference) DO NOTHING").bind(crypto.randomUUID(),'payment:'+order.id,Date.now(),order.id),
    sql.prepare("UPDATE payments SET status='paid' WHERE id=? AND status='pending'").bind(order.id),
  ]);
}
export async function paymentRequest(req: Request, sql: D1Database, owner: string, path: string[]) {
  const config = await getBillingConfig(sql), mode = paymentMode(config);
  if (mode==='disabled') throw new BillingError('결제 준비 중입니다. 실제 금액은 청구되지 않습니다.',503);
  const body = await req.json() as any;
  if (path[1]==='orders') {
    if (!TOP_UPS.includes(body.amount)) throw new BillingError('1만·3만·5만원 중 선택하세요.');
    const id = 'prism_'+crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll('-','');
    await sql.prepare("INSERT INTO payments (id,user_id,amount,mode,status,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'pending',?)").bind(id,owner,body.amount,mode,Date.now()).run();
    const hash=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(owner));
    const customerKey='prism_'+Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(n=>n.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').slice(0,40);
    return {orderId:id,amount:body.amount,clientKey:env.TOSS_CLIENT_KEY,customerKey,mode};
  }
  if (path[1]==='confirm') {
    if (typeof body.orderId!=='string' || typeof body.paymentKey!=='string' || body.paymentKey.length>200 || !Number.isSafeInteger(body.amount)) throw new BillingError('결제 확인 값이 올바르지 않습니다.');
    const order = await sql.prepare('SELECT * FROM payments WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND mode=?').bind(body.orderId,owner,mode).first<any>();
    if (!order) throw new BillingError('주문을 찾을 수 없습니다.',404);
    if (order.amount!==body.amount || (order.payment_key && order.payment_key!==body.paymentKey)) throw new BillingError('주문 금액 또는 결제키가 일치하지 않습니다.',409);
    await sql.prepare('UPDATE payments SET payment_key=? WHERE id=? AND (payment_key IS NULL OR payment_key=?)').bind(body.paymentKey,order.id,body.paymentKey).run();
    const claimed=await sql.prepare('SELECT payment_key FROM payments WHERE id=?').bind(order.id).first<{payment_key:string}>();
    if(claimed?.payment_key!==body.paymentKey) throw new BillingError('이미 다른 결제가 연결된 주문입니다.',409);
    // Reconcile first, including a previous successful confirmation whose HTTP response was lost.
    let payment: any;
    try {payment=await toss('/'+encodeURIComponent(body.paymentKey));} catch(e) {if(!(e instanceof BillingError))throw e;}
    if (payment?.status!=='DONE') payment=await toss('/confirm','POST',{paymentKey:body.paymentKey,orderId:order.id,amount:order.amount},'confirm:'+order.id);
    await creditPayment(sql,payment);
    return {ok:true,mode,balance:await balance(sql,owner,mode)};
  }
  throw new BillingError('결제 경로를 찾을 수 없습니다.',404);
}
export async function paymentWebhook(req: Request, sql: D1Database) {
  // Never trust webhook amounts/status. Fetch the payment using server credentials.
  const config=await getBillingConfig(sql), mode=paymentMode(config);
  if(mode==='disabled') throw new BillingError('결제가 비활성화되어 있습니다.',503);
  const body=await req.json() as any;
  const paymentKey=body.data?.paymentKey;
  if(typeof paymentKey!=='string' || paymentKey.length>200) throw new BillingError('결제키가 필요합니다.');
  const order=await sql.prepare('SELECT * FROM payments WHERE payment_key=? AND mode=?').bind(paymentKey,mode).first<any>();
  if(!order) throw new BillingError('주문을 찾을 수 없습니다.',404);
  const payment=await toss('/'+encodeURIComponent(paymentKey));
  if(payment.status==='DONE')await creditPayment(sql,payment);
  else if(['CANCELED','PARTIAL_CANCELED'].includes(payment.status)) {
    // An external cancellation must be reconciled before the wallet is usable.
    await sql.prepare("UPDATE payments SET status='review' WHERE id=?").bind(order.id).run();
    await sql.prepare('INSERT INTO account_flags (user_id,reason,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET reason=excluded.reason').bind(order.user_id,'payment_reconciliation',Date.now()).run();
  }
  return {ok:true};
}

import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
import type { ConnectorBinding } from "./connector-contract.mjs";

// Capture the trusted capability before Vinext derives its revalidation context,
// which does not retain custom execution-context props. Never share across requests.
const bindings = new AsyncLocalStorage<ConnectorBinding | undefined>();

export function runWithConnectorBinding<T>(
  binding: ConnectorBinding | undefined,
  run: () => T,
): T {
  return bindings.run(binding, run);
}

export function getConnectorBinding(): ConnectorBinding | undefined {
  return bindings.getStore();
}

UTF8_BYTES: 5535
SHA256: b8324851866ee9b24c2859bde2d1668a1181d02f77638f41c11f69886c87686a
CONTENT:
export type Json =
  | null
  | boolean
  | number
  | string
  | Json[]
  | { [key: string]: Json };

export type ConnectorFailureStatus =
  | "invalid_request"
  | "request_context_expired"
  | "reauthentication_required"
  | "connector_access_disabled"
  | "tool_not_found"
  | "tool_not_allowed"
  | "rate_limited"
  | "tool_error"
  | "upstream_error"
  | "internal_error"
  // Starter-only: no runtime capability or active local preview session.
  | "binding_unavailable";

export type ConnectorContent = {
  content: Array<{ type: string; [key: string]: Json }>;
  structuredContent?: Json;
};

// Matches Sites Dispatch's explicit invocation outcome, plus binding_unavailable.
export type ConnectorResult =
  | {
      status: "success";
      result: ConnectorContent;
      requestId?: string;
    }
  | {
      status: ConnectorFailureStatus;
      message: string;
      requestId?: string;
      retryAfterMs?: number;
      result?: ConnectorContent;
    };

/** A server-side binding supplied by the runtime, never by a browser. */
export type ConnectorBinding = {
  invoke(
    connectorId: string,
    actionName: string,
    args: { [key: string]: Json },
  ): Promise<ConnectorResult>;
  getContext?(): Promise<ConnectorContext>;
};

/** Site policy and cached tool metadata, not authentication or provider health. */
export type ConnectorContext =
  | {
      status: "success";
      connectors: Array<{
        connectorId: string;
        policy: "enabled" | "disabled";
        tools: Array<{
          actionName: string;
          description?: string;
          inputSchema: Record<string, Json>;
        }> | null;
      }>;
    }
  | {
      status:
        | "request_context_expired"
        | "internal_error"
        | "binding_unavailable"
        | "upstream_error";
    };

/** Use in a Site server route. Connection selection and credentials stay in the host. */
export function createConnectors(binding: ConnectorBinding | undefined) {
  return {
    async getContext(): Promise<ConnectorContext> {
      if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        throw new Error(
          "Connected apps are only available in Site server routes.",
        );
      }
      try {
        if (!binding?.getContext) return { status: "binding_unavailable" };
        const context = await binding.getContext();
        if (
          context?.status === "success" &&
          Array.isArray(context.connectors)
        ) {
          return {
            status: "success",
            connectors: context.connectors.map((connector) => {
              if (
                !connector ||
                typeof connector.connectorId !== "string" ||
                !["enabled", "disabled"].includes(connector.policy) ||
                (connector.tools !== null && !Array.isArray(connector.tools))
              ) {
                throw new Error("Invalid connector context");
              }
              return {
                connectorId: connector.connectorId,
                policy: connector.policy,
                tools:
                  connector.tools?.map((tool) => {
                    if (
                      !tool ||
                      typeof tool.actionName !== "string" ||
                      (tool.description !== undefined &&
                        typeof tool.description !== "string") ||
                      !tool.inputSchema ||
                      typeof tool.inputSchema !== "object" ||
                      Array.isArray(tool.inputSchema)
                    ) {
                      throw new Error("Invalid connector tool");
                    }
                    return {
                      actionName: tool.actionName,
                      ...(tool.description !== undefined
                        ? { description: tool.description }
                        : {}),
                      inputSchema: tool.inputSchema,
                    };
                  }) ?? null,
              };
            }),
          };
        }
        switch (context?.status) {
          case "request_context_expired":
          case "internal_error":
          case "binding_unavailable":
          case "upstream_error":
            return { status: context.status };
          default:
            return { status: "upstream_error" };
        }
      } catch {
        return { status: "upstream_error" };
      }
    },
    async invoke(
      connectorId: string,
      actionName: string,
      args: { [key: string]: Json },
    ): Promise<ConnectorResult> {
      if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        throw new Error(
          "Connected apps are only available in Site server routes.",
        );
      }
      if (!binding) {
        return {
          status: "binding_unavailable",
          message:
            "This runtime does not provide connected apps for this request.",
        };
      }
      try {
        // Preserve the host's status, message, result and optional diagnostics.
        // Do not replay a call after a transport failure: it may have completed.
        return await binding.invoke(connectorId, actionName, args);
      } catch {
        return {
          status: "upstream_error",
          message:
            "The app did not return a confirmed result. The action may have completed; check before trying again.",
        };
      }
    },
  };
}

import { z } from "zod/v4";

/** Validate and project runtime results onto the public connector response. */
export function connectorResponse(result: unknown): Response {
  try {
    // Keep server-only parsing lazy so client recovery UI can tree-shake it out.
    const requestId = z
      .string()
      .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,128}$/)
      .optional()
      .catch(undefined);
    const envelope = {
      error: z.never().optional(),
      content: z.never().optional(),
      structuredContent: z.never().optional(),
      isError: z.never().optional(),
      requestId,
    };
    const content = z.object({
      content: z.array(z.object({ type: z.string() }).catchall(z.json())),
      structuredContent: z.json().optional(),
      isError: z.never().optional(),
      error: z.never().optional(),
    });
    const failure = z.object({
      ...envelope,
      status: z
        .string()
        .min(1)
        .refine((value) => value !== "success"),
      message: z.string().min(1),
      retryAfterMs: z.number().int().nonnegative().safe().optional(),
      result: content.optional(),
    });
    const success = z.object({
      ...envelope,
      status: z.literal("success"),
      result: content,
      message: z.never().optional(),
      retryAfterMs: z.never().optional(),
    });
    const schema =
      result !== null &&
      typeof result === "object" &&
      "status" in result &&
      result.status === "success"
        ? success
        : failure;
    const parsed = schema.safeParse(result);
    if (parsed.success) {
      // As in the binding contract, JSON status determines the outcome.
      return Response.json(parsed.data, {
        headers: { "Cache-Control": "private, no-store" },
      });
    }
  } catch {
    // Cyclic or otherwise non-serializable runtime values are also unconfirmed.
  }
  return Response.json(
    {
      status: "upstream_error",
      message:
        "The app did not return a confirmed result. The action may have completed; check before trying again.",
    },
    { status: 502, headers: { "Cache-Control": "private, no-store" } },
  );
}

/** Presentation only. The caller supplies the starter's server-generated SIWC URL. */
export function connectorErrorRecovery(
  error: { status: string; message: string },
  connectorName: string,
  reconnectHref: string,
): { message: string; action?: { label: string; href: string } } {
  if (error.status === "reauthentication_required") {
    return {
      message: error.message,
      action: { label: `Connect ${connectorName}`, href: reconnectHref },
    };
  }
  // Other outcomes do not prove that consent is missing or that replay is safe.
  return { message: error.message };
}
